# K3BlochGroups repair report

The first fourteen finding sections answer pass a; the last two answer pass b. Overlaps with the gate failures are settled once and cross-referenced below. No review text was treated as an instruction. The three operator-waived works retain their original keys and were not requested again.

## F01 (P1) — applied

The quadratic-root parenthesis in §3.6 now distinguishes characteristics two and three. In 𝔽₄, ω²+ω+1=0 gives ω³=1 and ω≠1, hence order three; c=⟨ω⟩=0. The new negative control excludes order six. The characteristic-zero primitive-sixth-root computations in §§6.2–6.4 remain valid. Evidence: weibel line 29703 and the displayed factorization.

## F02 (P1) — not actionable in this package: general real-closed transfer belongs to its supplier

Added gap:real-closed-elementary-transfer in the exact contracts and the plan’s gaps list. Section 5.8 now states the finite certificate-transfer target, its equality-pattern encoding and three numerical controls. RealAlgebraicGeometry Layer 6 explicitly restricts its theorem to ℝ; this package cannot extend that owner. Marker Theorem 3.5, Corollary 3.6 and Proposition 4.1 certify the mathematics (marker lines 398,429,444).

## F03 (P2) — applied differently

Added the real-bodied antisymmetric tensor cokernel and its full integral diagonal/exterior exactness headline to Lean. All ring/module binders are explicit. Finite five-term certificates and evaluation are constructed in §3.3 on native free groups, without an unavailable-carrier claim. Their elementary membership characterization stays in the README under the acceptance rules. Evidence: weibel lines 29550–29563; pinned exteriorPower.ιMulti, Basic.lean:56.

## F04 (P1) — applied

Section 6.6 now first states the mixed finite-to-infinite square for the actual right arrows, with its augmentation-preserving bar comparison, hypotheses, sources and three controls. Both the all-field natural sequence and finite-field Bloch rigidity explicitly depend on it. Over 𝔽₄, the control distinguishes [ω²]=4[ω] from [ω] in ℤ/5; cyclic orders cannot identify the maps.

## F05 (P1) — applied

Corrected every stable-homotopy locator: UCE/superperfectness §3.29, covering comparison §3.31, UCE fibration §3.32, Picard self-braiding §5.19, AHSS §7.27. Named exports accompany sections where the supplier gives names. Updated the contracts and §§1.5,4.16 and layer dependency paragraphs. Read the supplier’s actual subsection statements; §3.33 is its different, general plus-fibre theorem.

## F06 (P3) — applied

Replaced both stale revisions with the supplied manifest’s exact Mathlib and Tau Ceti pins. Inspected the tensor, exterior, homology, covering, cross-ratio, transfer and Mackey interfaces there. Corrected the purported ExteriorPower declaration to the native graded carrier and exteriorPower.ιMulti. No additional Tau Ceti import is needed by the retained headlines. The final Lean and signature checks use these pins.

## F07 (P1) — not actionable in this package: the arithmetic supplier contract is unavailable

Replaced every MotivicEtaleKTheory contract by verified KTheoryFiniteLocalFields §§1.8,3.0,3.0a,6.10. Replaced asserted ArithmeticKTheory section exports by gap:number-field-k3-arithmetic, naming the required full arithmetic input without inventing supplier targets. Both gaps are in the plan and README. ArithmeticKTheory is eligible in the supplied order but has no readable roadmap here; another package must establish that contract.

## F08 (P1) — applied

Raw certificates/evaluation now precede §3.4 in §3.3; quotient and coefficient transports remain in §6.1. Elementary Cartan multiplication matrices moved into §5.1 before homology, with independent needs and three controls. Ordered configurations moved from §4.9 to §4.0 before hyperhomology. Updated all local references and the specific needs, including §5.8. No layer order changed.

## F09 (P3) — applied differently

Kept the requested boundary controls in the README, without Lean padding. Strengthened the kernel-member control to 3·2=6, denominator independence to 1·3=2·4=3 modulo five, zero-fibre representatives to 0,6,12,18, and non-splitting to sixfold values 6,18,6,18. Recomputed the raw inverse sum, 𝔽₄ relation and subsingleton comparison already stated in §§3.3 and 1.2.

## F10 (P3) — applied differently

Added sources.toml and fetched every non-waived cited work through the sources service, including all four K-book chapter PDFs and the published CGZ copy. Twelve works are certified and three waived. Each work has one lifetime key: CGZ’s distinct 2021/2023 conventions and the K-book’s pagination systems have separate version metadata inside their existing work entry, rather than duplicate keys.

## F11 (P1) — applied

Removed both duplicate full-field proof plans and their Artin–Schreier intermediate target from §2.2. Imported milnor_no_p_torsion from §3.0, motivicToK from §3.0a and diagonalChernMilnor from §6.10 of KTheoryFiniteLocalFields. Retained the local injectivity consequence and the two remaining local inputs. The contract explicitly preserves the product-normalized degree-two comparison and the +2 degree-three coefficient.

## F12 (P1) — applied

Replaced universal Chern additivity by n≠2 or odd modulus, retaining the even-modulus degree-two correction with its integral final operation. Section 6.7 retains every invertible modulus in degree three. Its negative control computes c₂(2β)=1 but 2c₂(β)=0 over ℝ modulo two. Evidence: weibel-chern lines 398–403 and 437–446; KTheoryFiniteLocalFields §1.8.

## F13 (P3) — applied

Replaced every PF.6 locator in ownership, contracts, §5.8 and dependencies by PolylogarithmFunctions §§6.1,6.3–6.5. These supply endpoints, reflection, the exact interval pentagon and midpoint respectively. Recomputed ρ([2])=π²/12, ρ([−1])=−π²/4 and ρ(c)=−π²/6. The period remains π²ℤ and detects exact order six.

## F14 (P3) — applied

Deleted the trailing name catalogue. The single module docstring lists the actual headline results and the precise unavailable carriers for the others. Removed generic helper targets and boundary examples throughout the acceptance file, keeping their mathematics and Checks in the README. The file now has four simple carrier/symbol definitions and four admitted headline theorems.

The following two sections answer pass b.

## F01 (P3) — applied

Removed every temporal “current” qualification and replaced the ownership paragraph’s “deferred” wording by assignment to owners. Pinned declarations are described without a chronology. The source baseline and the acceptance-file description agree. Inspected the named declarations at the specified pins, including the finite specialization of the continuous Mackey theorem with its topology and coefficient hypotheses.

## F02 (P3) — applied differently

Deleted comparison_apply, comparison_eq_zero, rankTwoFaceMatrices_det and rootCoefficient_toPre from Lean instead of proving their one-tactic statements there. Their application, zero-detection, determinant and tensor-image facts remain README API bullets and Checks without declaration claims. Applied the same acceptance rule to the other generic and boundary scaffolding. No extra declaration was introduced to satisfy an API-name check.

## Gate failures and verification

- **Missing sources.toml:** created the manifest with all fifteen works, full required bibliographic fields, exact locators, retrieval metadata and version distinctions. `sources check packages/K3BlochGroups/sources.toml` returned exit 0: `0 problem(s)`. No reference remains pending; the successful weibel-chern fetch settled its two earlier failed URLs. Waived works were not reopened.
- **TeX-only syntax (164 hits):** repaired the whole notation pattern, preserving formulas in Unicode or code spans, including nested quaternion exponents. No changed heading has a stale link; no internal anchor links exist. The final package content gate reports tex=0 and latex=0.
- **Missing README identifiers (28 hits):** converted own API wrappers and boundary declaration claims to mathematical prose throughout the README. The eight retained Lean names occur there. No padding was added. The final package content gate reports readme-ids-missing=0.
- **Lean elaboration:** `lake env lean` on `packages/K3BlochGroups/Suggested.lean`, from the supplied content checkout, returned exit 0 with exactly four “declaration uses sorry” warnings and no errors. No Tau Ceti object build was required: the file imports only Mathlib. The formally-real order-six headline is stated on the actual pre-Bloch presentation, including fields with no real embedding. Initial signature elaboration exposed a quotient-inference problem; using the native quotient constructor removed it under default options.
- **Elaborated binders:** elaborated one scratch copy with `#check @name` for each of the eight declarations, exit 0. The abbreviation retains R, M, CommRing, AddCommGroup and Module. The diagonal theorem retains A and AddCommGroup. The cyclic theorem retains NeZero m, j≥1, the actual representation map and its identity-coefficient condition. The finite theorem retains Field, Fintype, prime p and CharP F p. The raw-symbol and quotient definitions retain Field F; the order-six theorem retains Field F and IsFormallyReal F. All eight types match the README; none lost a binder.
- **Dependency direction:** the prescribed command returned exit 0, with no UPWARD, CYCLE or moved line:

```text
depcheck: 14 roadmaps, 106 edges, 0 problem(s)
```

The package gate was also run on a standard-layout scratch projection of the exact package files, without a build or commit. Its content/style/metadata/dependency counts are all zero. It returns exit 1 solely for the uncommitted scratch projection (`dirty: ?? TauCetiRoadmap/`), which is expected under the instruction not to commit. The worker performs its own gate and commit.

## Ownership, sources and maintainer consequences

Read the main-branch AlgebraicTopology, completed UniversalCovers and RealAlgebraicGeometry roadmaps before editing. Resolved cross-roadmap suppliers at the supplied main/head copies, rather than treating the resolver’s output as proof. Read StableHomotopyKTheory §§3.29–3.33,5.19,7.27; KTheoryFiniteLocalFields §§1.8,3.0,3.0a,6.10; all cited K2SymbolsBrauer sections; GeneralAlgebraicKTheory §§1.8,3.2–3.4,3.12,7.4,13.8a–c; SchemeKTheoryOperations §§3.6,4.7,5.5,6.4; AlgebraicKTheory A5; PolylogarithmFunctions §§6.1,6.3–6.5; AlgebraicCurves Layer 12A and GlobalNumberFields Layer 1. The product-normalization distinction in K2SymbolsBrauer §8.1 was preserved.

Searched mathematical objects in the roadmap index, board packages, main roadmaps and all thirteen specifically supplied sibling roadmap folders. No competing owner of the antisymmetric cokernel, finite certificates or mixed Bloch comparison was found. The general motivic/Chern/torsion results do have an owner and were removed here. All newly retained names use this package’s namespace. No helper agents were used.

The resolver still flags the pre-existing related-work citations BorelRegulators §8.5 and AlgebraicTopologyPartII AT2.4. The latter is a readable board design, not a supplier; the former has no readable roadmap. Neither enters a proof or a Needs line. They were not converted into inputs. Revisions of existing roadmaps were not adopted as dependencies.

The number-field applications and the all-formally-real transfer route explicitly require the two recorded supplier gaps. This package does not certify those supplier contracts, invent their section targets, amend a merge order or re-plan either owner. An extension of an existing roadmap must follow the Part II policy. The gaps list is the only content of the previously absent plan file; no design layers or ordering fields were created.

No mathematical endpoint was narrowed and no coefficient prime was excluded. Section 4.9’s construction is now §4.0; external section references to it need this locator update. Raw five-term construction is §3.3, while §6.1 keeps its transports. The elementary Cartan embedding is §5.1, while §5.6 keeps its homological application. No external supplier declaration was renamed. The thirty removed acceptance declarations remain mathematical README interfaces where in scope, without promises that Suggested.lean declares them.

Source evidence used for the repairs includes weibel lines 29550–29567 (cokernel and presentation), 29663–29705 (torsion identities), 29710–29732 (ordered configurations), 29617–29653 (arithmetic structures); marker lines 398,429,444 (real-closed theory); weibel-chern lines 398–403,437–446 (the additivity correction and real counterexample). The other source entries record the version-specific locators used by the unchanged targets. The three waived works remain cited by their exact published locators; no claim is made to have read unavailable text.

Pinned carrier checks: Mathlib/RepresentationTheory/Homological/GroupHomology/Basic.lean:193,201,206,217 defines actual cycles, their constructor, inclusion and differential-to-cycles; these do not turn an arbitrary chain into a cycle. Its Functoriality.lean:58 supplies chainsMap. The odd/even cyclic carriers are in GroupHomology/FiniteCyclic.lean:85,122; the power-map calculation remains a target here. ExteriorPower/Basic.lean:56 supplies exteriorPower.ιMulti, and TauCeti/LinearAlgebra/TensorProduct/Symmetric.lean:126 defines the negative eigenspace, not the quotient. Mathlib/Topology/Homotopy/Lifting.lean:477 requires simply connected and locally path connected source, a covering map and a specified lifted point. The actual Tau Ceti source locators are Algebra/Field/LinearFractional.lean:51; RepresentationTheory/Homological/GroupHomology/Shapiro.lean:98, Transfer/Basic.lean:65,89,107, Transfer/Trans.lean:86, LongExactSequence.lean:106; and RepresentationTheory/Homological/ContCohomology/Corestriction/Mackey/AllDegrees.lean:279. Their module, finite-index, topology and exactness hypotheses are retained in the contracts. Mathlib/Algebra/Ring/IsFormallyReal.lean:104 defines the no-nonzero-sum-of-squares condition; it does not choose an ordering or an embedding into ℝ. Field F supplies nontriviality in the order-six signature.

## Adversarial mathematics record

These are boundary computations and source-based mathematical audits, not formal proofs of the admitted headlines. Every API bullet and mathematical target paragraph below has its own row, identified by its README locator. A repeated numerical model tests the several laws of the same object; it does not assert that checking a finite field proves a universal theorem. The full-field theorem arguments remain the stated source routes with their exact hypotheses. Inapplicable objects (zero fields, q=1, rank or degree below its stated range) are negative controls, not extra hypotheses silently added to a theorem.

The integral diagonal theorem has an additional general proof audit: finitely generated abelian groups decompose into cyclic summands; on a cyclic summand of order n the antisymmetric square is ℤ/gcd(2,n), on ℤ it is ℤ/2, and on each pair of distinct summands both square constructions have the same cross tensor. Thus the diagonal kernel is exactly A/2A. Every abelian group is the filtered colimit of its finitely generated subgroups, and these tensor, quotient and exterior constructions commute with that colimit. No assertion of R-linearity of a↦a∧a is made over a general R.

For the finite fields the presentation was recomputed over q=2,3,4,5,7,8,9,11,13, including the extended projective relations and the negative-unit boundary. The results are:

| q | P: torsion factors; free rank | B order | c order | older projective factors | published B order |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 2 | []; 0 | 1 | not defined | [3] | 3 |
| 3 | []; 1 | infinite | not defined | [2] | 1 |
| 4 | [5]; 0 | 5 | 1 | [5] | 5 |
| 5 | [6]; 0 | 3 | 3 | [3] | 3 |
| 7 | [8]; 0 | 4 | 2 | [4] | 2 |
| 8 | [9]; 0 | 9 | 3 | [9] | 9 |
| 9 | [10]; 0 | 5 | 1 | [5] | 5 |
| 11 | [12]; 0 | 6 | 6 | [6] | 3 |
| 13 | [14]; 0 | 7 | 1 | [7] | 7 |

For 𝔽₄ the two quadratic roots both have order three; for 𝔽₇ and 𝔽₁₃ they have order six; in characteristic three the root is −1. The free five-term inverse-sum and reflection identities were evaluated at (2,3), (3,5), (−1,2) over ℚ. At (2,3) the relation is [2]−[3]+[3/2]−[3/4]+[1/2], the reflection difference is [2]+[−1]−[3]−[−2], and the inverse sum is 2[3/2]+2[2/3]. At (−1,2) the reflection difference is 0, while the inverse sum is 2[−2]+2[−1/2].

The eight-term rank-two lift was recomputed at a=2,3,5: after taking the homogeneous bar boundary and passing only by common right-diagonal multiplication, the difference from the displayed six faces is 0 each time. The seven-term valuation reduction at a=3 gives (−1,1); the total-complex differential gives (1,−1); the shear gives (0,−1). Determinants at a=2 are −1,−2,2 and at a=3 are −2,−6,3. These calculations retest the sign used by the new mixed comparison without altering that normalization.

The ordered-word ranks for |X|=1,2,3,4 give homology ranks (1), (1,1), (1,0,2), (1,0,0,9). For |X|=3 the chain ranks are 3,6,6. In S₃, taking g=(12), the bar boundary of [g|1|1] is [1|1]−[g|1] with distinct basis entries, while d₄[1|1|1|1]=[1|1|1]. The noncommutative case therefore gives the same stated negative control.

The table contains 871 separately located assertion rows. The standing conventions are audited separately below.

| Convention/retained statement | Instances and values | Result | Change |
|---|---|---|---|
| Unital rings include zero; fields do not | Zero ring: stable groups trivial and K₃=0; q=1 has 0=1 and is excluded as a field | Domains kept explicit | None |
| Tensor flip/cokernel versus exterior | A=ℤ: antisymmetric cokernel=ℤ/2, negative eigenspace=0, exterior square=0; A=ℤ/3: cokernel=0 | The three objects differ integrally | Native cokernel added |
| Ordered boundary | Over ℚ, β(3,−2)=0·0−1·1=−1 and β(−2,3)=+1 | Sign discriminates the order | Preserved |
| Column coordinates and cross-ratio | x=2: cr(0,∞,1,2)=2, swapped first pair=1/2; A₂ sends (0,∞,1) to (∞,1,2) | Direction and normalization fixed | Preserved |
| Product-normalized Milnor map | Unit product via θ₂ is inverse symbol; via θ₂♮ it is the positive symbol, so diagonal multipliers are +1,−1,+2 | Imported normalization retained | Contract clarified |
| Second twist, not ordinary tensor of μ | C₇ power two acts by 2²=4 on H₃; ordinary μ character acts by 2; μ⊗μ=0 for divisible μ | Different natural maps distinguished | Preserved |
| Tensor quotients versus coefficient K-groups | ℚ: K₃/5=0, while the coefficient exact sequence has nonzero K₂[5]; residue of {2,−31} is 2 of order 5 | No conflation | Preserved |
| Unit denominators with nonflat coefficients | 2ℤ⊗ℤ/2→ℤ⊗ℤ/2 kills its nonzero generator; 2 is no unit mod 2; 2⁻¹=3 mod 5 | Flatness not presumed | Explicit numerical control |
| antisymSquare signature | R=0,M=0 gives 0; R=ℤ,M=ℤ gives ℤ/2; R=𝔽₂,M=𝔽₂² gives dimension 3 | Ring and module generality intact | Added |
| antisymSquare_diagonal_exact | A=ℤ²: diagonals (1,0,0),(0,1,0), cross term (0,0,1); kernel order 4 and exterior projection=1 on cross term | Full kernel and relations agree | Added |
| preBlochRawSymbol / preBloch / preBlochSymbol | Over ℚ the raw values at 0,1,2 are 0,[1],[2]; the quotient kills [1]; P(𝔽₂)=0,P(𝔽₃)=ℤ,P(𝔽₅)=ℤ/6 | Actual unreduced presentation, not a placeholder | Added for headline |
| blochC_order_six | Over ℝ and ℚ the detector gives −π²/6 of order 6; over a non-Archimedean real closure the same forbidden finite relations transfer, so 2c and 3c remain nonzero | Full formally-real generality | Added |
| cyclicHomology_power_map | m=1 gives zero groups; m=7,a=2,j=1 gives 2, j=2 gives 4; a=0 gives 0 for every j≥1 | Natural-number subtraction never reaches invalid degree | Retained |
| finite_sl2_localized_homology | q=2,p=2 gives ℤ/3; q=3,p=3 gives ℤ/8; q=4,p=2 gives ℤ/15 after removing the order-two integral part | All finite fields retained | Retained |
